### Realignment context (brief)

Relative to the prior MAC football footprint, the 2026 membership reflects conference realignment and membership change:

- **Departure:** **Northern Illinois** left the MAC for the Mountain West (first MW season in 2026; documented in the companion Mountain West research package).
- **Addition:** **Sacramento State** joined as a **football-only** MAC member beginning July 1, 2026, transitioning from FCS (Big Sky era) to FBS. Non-football sports remain outside the MAC football alignment as described in school/conference announcements.
- **Bowl / championship eligibility (Sacramento State):** In June 2026 the NCAA Division I Cabinet eliminated the prior two-year postseason ban for FCS-to-FBS transitioning teams. Per the Sacramento State research file, the Hornets are therefore **immediately eligible** for bowl games, the MAC Championship, and potentially the CFP if usual FBS criteria are met.
- **UMass:** Listed as a full MAC member as of July 1, 2025 (UMass research file); competes in MAC football in 2026.

---

## 2. Methodology and Primary Source Types

Research for each team was compiled from publicly available sources accessed on or about **September 4–5, 2026**. Primary source types used across the conference package:

1. **Official athletics department rosters** (school `.edu` / athletics sites) — primary authority for jersey numbers, names, positions, hometowns/high schools, and academic eligibility class when published.
2. **Official or beat-reported depth charts** — e.g., BGSU Week-1 two-deep PDF, Miami (OH) Pitt game notes, Athens Messenger summary of Ohio game notes, Ball State camp report naming QB1.
3. **Ourlads.com** depth charts and numeric/alphabetical rosters — secondary depth and hometown cross-checks (update stamps vary by school; often July–September 2026).
4. **Beat / local media depth charts and coaching comments** — e.g., State Hornet (Sacramento State), Berkshire Eagle (UMass QB confirmation), ByTheBelt MAC previews, HERO Sports, Punt & Rally, ScoutSZN, Ypsilanti11.
5. **Recruiting databases and class trackers** — 247Sports, On3, ESPN recruiting, CFBTrack, school signing-day releases — used **only** when a specific rating was publicly verified for a named player.
6. **Secondary cross-checks** — ESPN / CBS roster pages, Wikipedia season/transition context, Phil Steele / school All-MAC releases where cited in team files.

**Policy enforced throughout:** If a recruiting star, high-school class year, or starter designation could not be verified in the sources consulted, the field is marked **Not listed** or the chart is labeled **projected**. No ratings or starter assignments were invented.

---

## 3. Cross-Conference Caveats

Readers should treat the attached materials as a **research snapshot**, not as an official Mid-American Conference or NCAA publication. The following limitations apply conference-wide:

| Caveat | Implication |
| --- | --- |
| **Stars incomplete for transfers and upperclassmen** | Composite / consensus stars are filled primarily for verified 2026 (and some earlier) high-school signees and a minority of returners/transfers with published bios. Most portal arrivals and multi-year veterans are **Not listed**. |
| **Many depth charts are projected, not official** | Bowling Green, Miami (OH), and Ohio have official / official-via-beat Week-1 two-deeps. Ball State, Sacramento State, and UMass are **MIXED** (some coach-confirmed starters; remainder projected). Akron, Buffalo, Central Michigan, Eastern Michigan, Kent State, Toledo, and Western Michigan charts are primarily **PROJECTED** (chiefly Ourlads ± beat). |
| **Jersey mismatches** | Numbers frequently differ across athletics sites, Ourlads, and media charts (shared numbers on large FBS rosters; media vs official conflicts). Prefer the school athletics roster for jersey when sources conflict (explicitly noted for Toledo and others). |
| **Position-battle volatility** | Open competitions and OR listings (e.g., Bowling Green LT/TE/HB; Kent State RB/OL; Ohio LB/CB co-starters; CMU early-season QB rotation) mean Week-1 actual lineups may diverge from mid/late-August projections. |
| **Roster churn / injuries** | Portal turnover (especially Toledo under Jacobs; Sacramento State under Carter), season-ending injuries (documented for CMU McIntosh / Joey VanWetzinga; Miami Gotkowski; Sac State Conklin hand), and eligibility rulings can change counts after the research date. |
| **Hometown / HS quirks** | Occasional official-site anomalies (blank HS fields, mismatched city/school pairs, spelling variants) are preserved as published rather than “corrected.” |
| **Eligibility labeling** | Some schools (e.g., Ball State 1st–5th year; Eastern Michigan Y-#) use newer NCAA eligibility models; labels are preserved as published. |
| **FCS→FBS (Sacramento State)** | First FBS / first MAC football season; postseason eligibility notes are documented in the Sacramento State team file. |

## 5. How to Read the Attached Team Files

Each school file in `/workspace/mac-2026/` follows a consistent research structure:

1. **Header** — Conference affiliation, head coach, home venue (when stated), access/research date.
2. **Sources table** — URLs and access dates for every primary source used for that school.
3. **Starter Depth Chart** — Offense, defense, and specialists, with an explicit **projected vs official** caveat at the top of the section.
4. **Full Roster** — Player tables with columns for Name, Stars, Class Year, Position, High School / Hometown, and Notes (when present). Gaps are preserved as **Not listed**.
5. **Data gaps / caveats** — School-specific limitations (injuries, eligibility murk, jersey conflicts, FCS→FBS notes, etc.).

### Companion documents in this package

| File | Contents |
| --- | --- |
| `00-executive-overview.md` | This overview (scope, methodology, caveats, snapshot table) |
| `01-starter-depth-charts-summary.md` | Consolidated starter charts only, with roster-linked Stars / Class Year / HS-Hometown when present in source tables |
| `MAC-2026-Football-Rosters-Complete.md` | Master volume: overview + starter summary + full text of all thirteen team files (alphabetical by school) |
| `akron.md` … `western-michigan.md` | Individual school research files (source of truth for player-level detail) |

### Reading conventions

- Depth-chart **Status** labels (**OFFICIAL**, **MIXED**, **PROJECTED**) appear at the top of each school section in the starter summary.
- **Not listed** means the field was not verified — it does **not** mean “unrated” in a recruiting-service sense, nor that the player is unimportant.
- **Class Year** may mean academic eligibility (Fr/So/Jr/Sr / R-Sr / 5th / Y-#) as published on athletics rosters, or HS recruiting/signing year when that is how the school file recorded it — see each file’s roster preamble.
- Prefer **official athletics spelling** when media spellings diverge (documented in individual caveats).
- Starter-summary enrichment pulls Stars / Class Year / HS-Hometown **only** from that school’s full roster table when a name match is found; unmatched depth-chart names retain **Not listed** gaps.
- **Miami (OH)** denotes Miami University (Oxford, Ohio) — MAC — **not** the University of Miami (Florida / ACC).

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **No official school depth chart** located; Ourlads is projected and shifted after the 9/3 Wake Forest opener (QB: Roggow listed over preseason starter Poffenbarger).
2. **Recruiting stars** mostly available only for 2026 HS/JC enrollees; returning players and most transfers marked **Not listed** (historical HS ratings not pulled player-by-player from 247/On3 archives).
3. Heavy portal turnover (~40 scholarship replacements per Moorhead); roster composition can change weekly.
4. Dual jersey numbers are intentional (offense/defense share numbers).
5. Omari Abor weight listed as null on athletics site.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Official Week 1 depth chart is authoritative** for starters; Ourlads (Aug 5) is outdated relative to school release and should not override "or" battles on the official sheet.
2. Official chart uses many **"or"** designations (LT, TE, HB, P, PK, LS, returns) — true Week 1 starters may still be undecided.
3. Recruiting stars documented primarily for 2026 HS class; most returning players/transfers **Not listed**.
4. Dual jersey numbers throughout; some depth-chart spellings differ slightly from roster (e.g., Carsen vs Carson Melvin; Drey/Dray Braxton).
5. FB Leo Kemp appears on roster/Ourlads but not on official Week 1 two-deep offense table.
6. Isaiah Hayward (3★ WR commit on CFBTrack class board) **not** found on live bgsufalcons.com roster — commit status unclear; omitted from roster table.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Recruiting stars:** Complete 247Sports/On3 composite stars available mainly for 2025–2026 HS signees; most upperclassmen and portal transfers listed as **"Not listed"** (no reliable public composite found for this research pass). Where shown, ratings are 247Sports composite-style (e.g., 0.87x ≈ 3★).
2. **No official school depth chart** published on ubbulls.com at research time — starter designations are Ourlads/HERO, not UB Athletics.
3. **Jersey number collisions** are intentional (offense/defense share numbers).
4. **Class year labels** follow the athletics site (Fr./So./Jr./Sr./5th/6th); "Recruitment/class year" is inferred from standing + transfer history, not a signed LOI year for every player.
5. Ourlads HC line historically lagged (search snippets once showed Maurice Linguist); current Ourlads page correctly lists **Pete Lembo**.
6. Some freshmen (e.g., Blake Sholder) lack major/HS detail beyond athletics listing.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Stars:** Mix of 247Sports composite (0.xxx) for 2026 commits and 247 roster “Rating” integers (≈80–89 = 3★, ~89–90 = high 3★/low 4★). Many transfers/JUCO show **NA / Not listed**. Tommy McIntosh 89 and Joey VanWetzinga 89 flagged as **4★** per 247 roster page scale.
2. **Injury overrides:** McIntosh (ACL) and Joey VanWetzinga (season) per By The Belt — depth chart above notes this; Ourlads snapshot may lag.
3. **No official CMU depth chart** on athletics site at research time.
4. **Ourlads update 08/12/2026** predates Week 1 (CMU @ New Mexico Sept 5) — treat as preseason unofficial.
5. Collin Payne HS listed oddly on athletics site (Golden West C.C. as HS field) — previous school El Toro H.S. retained in table.
6. Dominique Maiava moved OL→DL per preview; still listed DL on official roster.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Stars:** CFBTrack/247 list ~24 HS commits as mostly **3★**; table shows known 2026 class ratings. Vast majority of upperclassmen/transfers = **"Not listed"** (no reliable composite found).
2. **No official EMU depth chart** on emueagles.com — Ourlads (09/02/2026) used as unofficial Week 1–2 proxy.
3. **Y-# vs traditional class:** Both shown; Y-# is EMU’s authoritative eligibility label for 2026.
4. Jersey number sharing (offense/defense) is intentional.
5. Beat piece (Ypsilanti11) notes Noah Kim on 7th-year waiver context — athletics still lists Gr. / Y-5.
6. Fa'Rel Carter appears on some recruiting lists but **not** on current official EMU roster page — omitted (not invented).
7. PuntAndRally cites ~93 roster size; official athletics page count used here (113).

# Sacramento State Hornets — 2026 Football Roster & Depth Chart

**As of:** September 4, 2026 (PT)  
**Conference:** Mid-American Conference (MAC) — football-only member  
**Head Coach:** Alonzo Carter (1st season)  
**Record entering Sept. 5:** 0–1 (0–1 MAC) — lost opener at Eastern Michigan 17–28 (Aug. 29)

## Context: FCS → FBS transition

Sacramento State is a **new MAC football-only member** for 2026, transitioning from FCS (Big Sky era; 33 years FCS 1993–2025) to FBS. The Hornets replace Northern Illinois in the MAC for football. Non-football sports remain outside the MAC football alignment as described in school/conference announcements; football joined for a multi-year term beginning July 1, 2026.

**Eligibility / postseason caveat:** In June 2026 the NCAA Division I Cabinet eliminated the prior two-year postseason ban for FCS-to-FBS transitioning teams. Sacramento State is therefore **immediately eligible** for bowl games, the MAC Championship, and potentially the CFP if it meets the usual FBS criteria (typically at least 6–6). Entry costs reported around ~$23–24M over five years ($5M NCAA + ~$18M MAC).

**Coaching / scheme notes:** OC Eric Kiesau; DC Adam Clark; Ourlads lists Multiple offense / 3-4 defense (Wikipedia also notes 3–3–5 base). Large portal class under Carter after Brennan Marion left for Colorado.

## Data gaps / caveats

- **No official school depth chart** published on hornetsports.com at time of research; starter chart is **Ourlads projected** (Aug. 25) plus beat adjustments.
- **Star ratings incomplete:** Full 247/On3/ESPN composite not available for every roster player. Stars filled only when found in school signing release or recruiting writeups; otherwise **Not listed**. Many JC/portal players lack current composite stars.
- **Jersey duplicates** are intentional (offense/defense share numbers).
- **Class/exp labels** follow athletics site (Jr./Sr./TR/JC/1V etc.); Ourlads sometimes differs (e.g., RS designations).
- **#43 John Bloomfield** is a memorial listing, not an active player.
- Roster is early-season (Week 1–2); depth will change with injuries (Conklin hand) and performance.
- Wikipedia roster section incomplete vs official site — prefer hornetsports.com.

# Toledo Rockets — 2026 Football Roster & Depth Chart

**As of:** September 4, 2026 (PT)  
**Conference:** Mid-American Conference (MAC)  
**Head Coach:** Mike Jacobs (1st season at Toledo; previously Mercer)  
**Record entering Sept. 4 night game:** Season opener at Michigan State (FS1); media notes heavy roster turnover under new staff.

## Context

First year under HC Mike Jacobs after Jason Candle departed (multiple players followed Candle to UConn). Official media notes: only **one returning starter** from 2025 (**Stephen Gales**, OL); **21 starters lost**; large transfer class including **14 from Mercer**, ranked **No. 1 MAC transfer portal class** by 247Sports (36 transfers announced). Phil Steele preseason All-MAC: Zock & Griffith (1st), Dabbs/Gales/Hutchinson/Hammer (2nd), Carwise/Fox (3rd), Miller/Watkins (4th).

**Scheme:** Ourlads — Spread offense / 4-2-5 defense.

## Data gaps / caveats

- **Depth chart is PROJECTED (Ourlads July 16)** — season already underway Week 1; actual Michigan State game-day chart may differ. No full official depth chart HTML found on utrockets.com beyond game notes.
- **Star ratings mostly Not listed** for returning players and many transfers; 247 transfer ratings (~0.82–0.86) noted where found on commits page; HS 3★ noted for selected 2026 signees from NSD release / 247.
- **Ourlads vs official jersey mismatches** (Walendzak, Osborn, etc.) — official roster used for full table.
- **Thomas Zsiros** on Ourlads WR depth but **absent from official roster** — gap.
- Extreme roster churn (Jacobs Year 1 + Candle exits) means early-season depth is unusually fluid.
- Carter Fouty / Mike Drennen II appear on some third-party lists historically; **not** on current official 2026 athletics roster scrape — omitted.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Stars sparse for veterans:** Many returning/transfer players lack published 247/On3 HS composites on school pages; marked **Not listed** rather than guessed.
2. **Depth chart is PROJECTED:** Ourlads post–Rutgers (9/3/2026). No public official UMass two-deep PDF found for Week 1 beyond game notes narrative.
3. **QB depth fluid:** Coach confirmed Watson III as starter; backups (Inagawa/Perry/McCarthy/McCutcheon) remain competitive per Berkshire Eagle (8/19/2026).
4. **RB chart shift:** Ourlads moved Faulkner to RB1 after opener; Washington listed on reserves — injury/rotation unclear without official injury report detail.
5. **Jersey overlaps:** Multiple shared numbers (e.g., 0, 1, 2) — normal offense/defense pairing.
6. **247Sports enrollee list vs athletics:** Some 2026 signees appear on athletics with different HS names (e.g., Drew Alsup listed Central Catholic on athletics vs Williston Northampton on 247 in places) — athletics hometown/HS used as primary.
7. **Transfer ratings** are portal evaluations, not HS stars; both shown when available.
8. **Player count 102** unique players from official UMass Athletics roster table as of research date; walk-ons may be added midseason.

## Data Gaps / Caveats

1. **Depth chart PROJECTED only:** Ourlads last updated **08/12/2026** (preseason). No official WMU Athletics two-deep found for early September 2026; treat as projection.
2. **Stars for returners/transfers:** Vast majority of upperclassmen and Power-conference transfers lack published HS star on athletics pages — marked **Not listed**. 2026 HS/JUCO class largely **consensus 3★** per WMU release / CFBTrack / MLive.
3. **Name variants:** Athletics lists **Lolo Mataele**; Ourlads uses **Ofa Mataele** — same player (#24 RB). **Tre Johnson** = Philip “Tre” Johnson on signing release.
4. **Chad Schuster previous school:** Athletics lists “Syracuse” in previous-school column; Mitten Football treats him as returning OL starter — prior path may be HS/transfer hybrid; left as athletics lists.
5. **Jersey overlaps:** Shared numbers across offense/defense (0, 1, 2, 4, 6, etc.) are normal.
6. **Player count 104** from official athletics roster (all listed); Punt & Rally cited ~81 scholarship-focused earlier — difference is walk-ons / specialists / depth.
7. **OL continuity vs Ourlads LT:** Beat reporting emphasizes returning starters Schleicher, Dabo, Schuster, Whitenack; Ourlads projects **Brandon Smith (TR)** at LT — projection may differ from camp depth.
8. **No Week 1 official injury/depth PDF** located during research; update after first MAC game notes if needed.
